E poi ti lasceremo.<br>Intanto tu e Peter potete continuare a parlare<br>E io non vi disturberò. |  
| EDWARD | Mio caro Alex,<br><br><br>In dispensa non ci sarà nulla degno della tua cucina.<br>Non se ne parla nemmeno. |  
| ALEX | Ah, ma è proprio quello il mio dono speciale —<br><br><br>Confezionare un pasto squisito con nulla.<br>Bastano gli avanzi che hai. L'ho imparato in Oriente.<br>Con una manciata di riso e un po' di pesce secco<br>So fare una mezza dozzina di piatti. Non dire una parola.<br>Comincio subito. |  
| *(didascalia)* | [Esce verso la cucina] |  
| EDWARD | [[motifs/well\|Dunque]], dov'eri rimasto? |  
| PETER | Mi hai chiesto come sono arrivato a conoscere [[characters/celia\|Celia]].<br><br><br>L'ho incontrata qui, circa un anno fa. |  
| EDWARD | A uno dei giovedì dilettanteschi di [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]? |  
| PETER | Un [[thursday\|giovedì]]. Perché dici dilettantesco? |  
| EDWARD | I tentativi di [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] di aprire un salotto,<br><br><br>Dove io intrattenevo gli ospiti minori<br>E mi occupavo degli spostati, gli errori di [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]].<br>Ma tu eri uno dei successi minori<br>Per un [[motifs/time\|po']] almeno. |  
| PETER | Non direi questo.<br><br><br>Ma [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] fu di una gentilezza straordinaria con me<br>E le devo moltissimo. E poi conobbi [[characters/celia\|Celia]].<br>Era diversa da qualunque ragazza avessi mai conosciuto<br>E non facile da avvicinare, in quell'occasione. |  
| EDWARD | La vedevi spesso?<br><br><br>VOCE DI ALEX. Edward, hai una pentola a [[topoi/double\|bagnomaria]]? |  
| EDWARD | Immagino che ci debba essere una pentola a [[topoi/double\|bagnomaria]]:<br><br><br>Non ce n'è una in ogni cucina?<br>VOCE DI ALEX.                                 Non riesco a trovarla.<br><br><br>Ecco un'altra sorpresa svanita. Devo pensarne un'altra. |  
| PETER | Non molto spesso.<br><br><br>E quando la vedevo, non avevo occasione di parlarle. |  
| EDWARD | Tu e [[characters/celia\|Celia]] eravate invitati per scopi diversi.<br><br><br>La tua parte era d'essere una delle scoperte di [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]];<br>Quella di [[characters/celia\|Celia]], di fornire mondanità e moda.<br>[[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] ha sempre avuto l'[[concepts/ambition\|ambizione]]<br>D'affermarsi in due mondi insieme —<br>Ma doveva essere lei stessa il legame fra i due.<br>Ecco perché, credo, i suoi giovedì furono un fallimento. |  
| PETER | Parli come se tutto fosse finito. |  
| EDWARD | Oh no, no, tutto resta incompiuto.<br><br><br>Ma non mi hai detto come sei arrivato a conoscere [[characters/celia\|Celia]]. |  
| PETER | La rividi qualche giorno dopo<br><br><br>[[Alone\|Sola]] a un concerto. E io ero [[Alone\|solo]].<br>Sono sempre andato ai concerti [[Alone\|solo]] —<br>Dapprima perché non conoscevo nessuno con cui andarci,<br>E poi scoprii che preferivo andarci [[Alone\|solo]].<br>Ma una ragazza come [[characters/celia\|Celia]], parve stranissimo,<br>Perché la consideravo soltanto un nome<br>In una rubrica mondana, trovarla lì [[Alone\|sola]].<br>Comunque, attaccammo discorso<br>E scoprii che andava ai concerti [[Alone\|sola]]<br>E a guardare i quadri. Così ci incontravamo spesso<br>Allo stesso modo, e a volte ci andavamo insieme.<br>Ed essere con [[characters/celia\|Celia]] era qualcosa di diverso<br>Dalla compagnia o dalla [[concepts/solitude\|solitudine]]. E a volte prendevamo il tè<br>E una volta o due cenammo insieme. |  
| EDWARD | E dopo di allora<br><br><br>Ti presentò mai alla sua famiglia<br>O a qualcuno dei suoi amici? |  
| PETER | No, ma una volta o due me ne parlò<br><br><br>E della loro mancanza d'interessi intellettuali. |  
| EDWARD | E che cosa accadde dopo? |  
| PETER | Oh, non accadde nulla.<br><br><br>Ma pensavo che tenesse davvero a me.<br>Ed ero così felice quando stavamo insieme —<br>Così… appagato, così… in pace: non so esprimerlo;<br>Non avevo mai immaginato una felicità così quieta.<br>Avevo conosciuto solo l'eccitazione, il delirio,<br>Il [[motifs/desire\|desiderio]] di possesso. Non era affatto così.<br>Era qualcosa di stranissimo. C'era una tale… tranquillità… |  
| EDWARD | E che cosa ha interrotto questa interessante relazione? |  
| *(didascalia)* | [Entra ALEX in maniche di camicia e con un grembiule] |  
| ALEX | Edward, non trovo il curry in polvere. |  
| EDWARD | Non c'è curry in polvere. [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] detesta il curry. |  
| ALEX | Ecco un'altra sorpresa svanita, allora. Devo pensarci.<br><br><br>Non mi aspettavo di trovare dei manghi,<br>Ma sul curry in polvere contavo. |  
| *(didascalia)* | [Esce] |  
| PETER | È esattamente ciò che voglio sapere.<br><br><br>È semplicemente sbiadita — dentro qualche altra immagine —<br>Come una dissolvenza. Non vuole vedermi;<br>Trova scuse, non molto plausibili,<br>E quando la vedo, sembra assorta<br>In qualche segreta eccitazione che non posso condividere. |  
| EDWARD | Credi che abbia semplicemente perso interesse per te? |  
| PETER | La metti proprio male. Io la vedo diversamente.<br><br><br>Non è il suo interesse per me che mi manca —<br><br><br>Ma quei momenti in cui pareva che condividessimo una percezione,<br>Un sentimento, un'esperienza indefinibile<br>In cui eravamo entrambi inconsapevoli di noi stessi.<br>Nei tuoi termini, forse, ha perso interesse per me. |  
| EDWARD | È tutto normalissimo. Se solo tu sapessi<br><br><br>Quanto sei fortunato. Fra poco<br>Questa sarebbe diventata una relazione qualunque<br>Come tutte le altre. Al raffreddarsi della febbre<br>Avresti scoperto che lei era un'altra donna<br>E che tu eri un altro uomo. Mi congratulo con te<br>Per una fuga tempestiva. |  
| PETER | Preferirei che mi fossero risparmiate<br><br><br>Le tue congratulazioni. Dovevo parlare con qualcuno.<br>E ti ho raccontato di qualcosa di reale —<br>La mia prima esperienza della realtà<br>E forse è l'ultima. E tu non capisci. |  
| EDWARD | Mio caro Peter, ti ho soltanto detto<br><br><br>Che cosa ti sarebbe accaduto con [[characters/celia\|Celia]]<br>Nel giro d'altri sei mesi di [[motifs/time\|tempo]]. Ecco tutto.<br>Puoi prenderlo o lasciarlo. |  
| PETER | Ma che cosa devo fare? |  
| EDWARD | Nulla. Aspetta. Torna in California. |  
| PETER | Ma devo vedere [[characters/celia\|Celia]]. |  
| EDWARD | Sarà la stessa [[characters/celia\|Celia]]?<br><br><br>Meglio accontentarsi della [[characters/celia\|Celia]] che ricordi.<br>Ricordi! Ti dico che è già un [[motifs/memory\|ricordo]]. |  
| PETER | Ma devo vedere [[characters/celia\|Celia]] almeno per farmi dire da lei<br><br><br>Che cosa è accaduto, nei suoi termini. Finché non lo saprò<br>Non conoscerò la [[concepts/truth\|verità]] neppure sul [[motifs/memory\|ricordo]].<br>Condividevamo davvero quegli interessi? Sentivamo davvero lo stesso<br><br><br>Quando udivamo certa musica? O guardavamo certi quadri?<br><br><br>C'era qualcosa di reale. Ma qual è la realtà… |  
| *(didascalia)* | [Squilla il telefono] |  
| EDWARD | Mi scusi un momento. |  
| *(didascalia)* | [Al telefono] |  
| EDWARD |                                                        Pronto!… Non posso parlare adesso…<br>Sì, c'è… [[motifs/well\|Bene]], allora ti richiamo<br>Appena posso.<br>[A PETER] Scusami. Dicevi? |  
| PETER | Dicevo: qual è la realtà<br><br><br>D'un'esperienza fra due persone irreali?<br>Se solo posso tenermi stretto il [[motifs/memory\|ricordo]]<br>Potrò sopportare qualunque futuro. Ma devo scoprire<br>La [[concepts/truth\|verità]] sul passato, per amore del [[motifs/memory\|ricordo]]. |  
| EDWARD | Non c'è [[motifs/memory\|ricordo]] che tu possa avvolgere nella canfora<br><br><br>Senza che le tarme ci entrino. Dunque vuoi vedere [[characters/celia\|Celia]].<br>Non so perché mi stia prendendo tutto questo disturbo<br>Per proteggerti dallo [[archetypes/fool\|sciocco]] che sei.<br>Che cosa vuoi che faccia? |  
| PETER | Vedi [[characters/celia\|Celia]] per me.<br><br><br>Tu la conosci in un modo diverso dal mio<br>E sei molto più vecchio. |  
| EDWARD | Molto più vecchio? |  
| PETER | Sì, sono certo che darebbe ascolto a te<br><br><br>Come a qualcuno disinteressato. |  
| EDWARD | [[motifs/well\|Bene]], vedrò [[characters/celia\|Celia]]. |  
| PETER | Grazie, Edward. Sei molto buono. |  
| *(didascalia)* | [Entra ALEX, con la giacca indosso] |  
| ALEX | Oh, Edward! Ti ho preparato una tale delizia!<br><br><br>Credo davvero che fra tutti i miei trionfi<br>Questo sia il più grande. Fare qualcosa dal nulla!<br>Mai, nemmeno viaggiando in Albania,<br>Ho ricavato una cena simile da così pochi materiali<br>Come quelli che ho trovato nel tuo frigorifero. Ma naturalmente<br>Ho avuto la fortuna di trovare una mezza dozzina d'uova. |  
| EDWARD | Come! Hai usato tutte quelle uova! La zia di [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]<br><br><br>Le ha appena mandate dalla campagna. |  
| ALEX | Ah, dunque la zia<br><br><br>Esiste davvero. Una prova sostanziosa. |  
| EDWARD | No, no… voglio dire, questa è un'altra zia. |  
| ALEX | Capisco. La zia vera. Ma mi sarai grato.<br><br><br>Ci sono pochissimi contadini in Montenegro<br>Che possano avere il piatto che mangerai, di questi tempi. |  
| EDWARD | Ma la mia colazione? |  
| ALEX | Non preoccuparti della colazione.<br><br><br>Tutto ciò che ti occorre è una tazza di caffè nero<br>E un po' di pane tostato. L'ho lasciato a sobbollire.<br>Non lasciarlo più d'altri dieci minuti.<br>Ora vado, e mi porto via Peter. |  
| PETER | Edward, ti ho rubato troppo [[motifs/time\|tempo]],<br><br><br>E tu vuoi restare [[Alone\|solo]]. Porta i miei [[motifs/love\|saluti]] a [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]<br>Quando torna… ma, se non ti dispiace,<br>Preferirei che non le dicessi ciò che ti ho detto. |  
| EDWARD | Non dirò nulla di tutto questo a [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]. |  
| PETER | Grazie, Edward. Buona [[motifs/night\|notte]]. |  
| EDWARD | Buona [[motifs/night\|notte]], Peter,<br><br><br>E buona [[motifs/night\|notte]], Alex. Oh, e se non vi dispiace,<br>Chiudete la porta uscendo, in modo che scatti. |  
| ALEX | Ricorda, Edward, non più di dieci minuti, Venti minuti, e la mia opera sarà rovinata. |  
| *(didascalia)* | [Escono ALEX e PETER] |  
| *(didascalia)* | [EDWARD prende il telefono e compone un numero] |  
| EDWARD | C'è la signorina [[characters/celia-coplestone\|Celia Coplestone]]? … Quanto tempo fa? …<br><br><br>No, non importa.<br>SIPARIO |



<span class="atom-split" data-atom="act_1--scene_2" data-title="The Cocktail Party — Act I, Scene 2" data-chapter="Scene 2" data-kind="scene" data-tags="motif/blood,motif/desire,motif/home,motif/love,motif/night,motif/time,motif/well,concept/doubt,concept/freedom,concept/hope,concept/self,concept/vanity,character/celia,character/julia,character/lavinia"></span>


| Speaker | Line |
|---|---|
| *(didascalia)* | The same room: a quarter of an hour later. EDWARD is [[Alone\|alone]], playing Patience. The doorbell rings, and he goes to answer it.<br>[[characters/celia\|CELIA]]’S VOICE. Are you [[Alone\|alone]]? |
| *(didascalia)* | [EDWARD returns with [[characters/celia\|CELIA]]] |
| EDWARD | [[characters/celia\|Celia]]! Why have you come back?<br><br><br>I said I would telephone as soon as I could:<br>And I tried to get you a short while ago. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | If there had happened to be anyone with you<br><br><br>I was going to say I’d come back for my umbrella….<br>I must say you don’t seem very pleased to see me.<br>Edward, I understand what has happened<br>But I could not understand your manner on the telephone.<br>It did not seem like you. So I felt I must see you.<br>Tell me it’s all right, and then I’ll go. |
| EDWARD | But how can you say you understand what has happened?<br><br><br>I don’t know what has happened, or what is going to happen;<br><br><br>And to try to understand it, I want to be [[Alone\|alone]]. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | I should have thought it was perfectly simple.<br><br><br>[[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] has left you. |
| EDWARD | Yes, that was the situation.<br><br><br>I suppose it was pretty obvious to everyone. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | It was obvious that the aunt was a pure invention<br><br><br>On the spur of the moment, and not a very good one.<br>You should have been prepared with something better, for [[characters/julia\|Julia]];<br>But it doesn’t really matter. They will know soon enough.<br>Doesn’t that settle all our difficulties? |
| EDWARD | It has only brought to light the real difficulties. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | But surely, these are only temporary.<br><br><br>You know I accepted the situation<br>Because a [[divorce]] would ruin your career;<br>And we thought that [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] would never want to leave you.<br>Surely you don’t hold to that silly convention<br>That the husband must always be the one to be divorced?<br>And if she chooses to give you the grounds … |
| EDWARD | I see. But it is not like that at all.<br><br><br>[[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] is coming back. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] coming back!<br><br><br>Do you mean to say that she’s laid a trap for us? |
| EDWARD | No. If there is a trap, we are all in the trap,<br><br><br>We have set it for ourselves. But I do not know<br>What kind of a trap it is. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Then what has happened? |
| *(didascalia)* | [The telephone rings] |
| EDWARD | Damn the telephone. I suppose I must answer it.<br><br><br>Hello … oh, hello! … No. I mean yes, Alex;<br>Yes, of course … it was marvellous.<br>I’ve never tasted anything like it …<br>Yes, that’s very interesting. But I just wondered<br>Whether it mightn’t be rather indigestible? …<br>Oh, no, Alex, don’t bring me any [[cheese]];<br>I’ve got some [[cheese]] … No, not Norwegian;<br>But I don’t really want [[cheese]] … Slipper what? …<br>Oh, from Jugoslavia … prunes and alcohol?<br>No, really, Alex, I don’t want anything.<br>I’m very tired. Thanks awfully, Alex.<br>Good [[motifs/night\|night]]. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | What on earth was that about? |
| EDWARD | That was Alex. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | I know it was Alex.<br><br><br>But what was he talking of? |
| EDWARD | I had quite forgotten.<br><br><br>He made his way in, a little while ago,<br>And insisted on cooking me something for supper;<br>And he said I must eat it within ten minutes.<br>I suppose it’s still cooking. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | You suppose it’s still cooking!<br><br><br>I thought I noticed a peculiar smell:<br>Of course it’s still cooking — or doing something.<br>I must go and investigate. |
| *(didascalia)* | [Starts to leave the room] |
| EDWARD | For heaven’s sake, don’t bother! |
| *(didascalia)* | [Exit [[characters/celia\|CELIA]]] |
| EDWARD | Suppose someone came and found you in the kitchen? |
| *(didascalia)* | [EDWARD goes over to the table and inspects his game of Patience. He moves a card. The doorbell rings repeatedly. Re-enter [[characters/celia\|CELIA]], in an apron.] |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | You’d better answer the door, Edward.<br><br><br>It’s the best thing to do. Don’t lose your head.<br>You see, I really did leave my umbrella;<br>And I’ll say I found you here starving and helpless<br>And had to do something. Anyway, I’m staying<br>And I’m not going to hide. |
| *(didascalia)* | [Returns to kitchen. The bell rings again. EDWARD goes to front door, and is heard to say:] |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] |  [[characters/julia\|Julia]]!<br>What have you come back for? |
| *(didascalia)* | [Enter [[characters/julia\|JULIA]]] |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | I’ve had an inspiration! |
| *(didascalia)* | [Enter [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] with saucepan] |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Edward, it’s ruined! |
| EDWARD | What a good thing. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | But it’s ruined the saucepan too. |
| EDWARD | And half a dozen eggs:<br><br><br>I wanted one for breakfast. A boiled egg.<br>It’s the only thing I know how to cook. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | [[characters/celia\|Celia]]! I see you’ve had the same inspiration<br><br><br>That I had. Edward must be fed.<br>He’s under such a strain. We must keep his strength up.<br>Edward! Don’t you realise how lucky you are<br>To have two Good Samaritans? I never heard of that before. |
| EDWARD | The man who fell among thieves was luckier than I:<br><br><br>He was left at an inn. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | Edward, how ungrateful.<br><br><br>What’s in that saucepan? |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Nobody knows. |
| EDWARD | It’s something that Alex came and prepared for me.<br><br><br>He would do it. Three Good Samaritans.<br><br><br>I forgot all about it. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | But you mustn’t touch it. |
| EDWARD | Of course I shan’t touch it. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | My dear, I should have warned you:<br><br><br>Anything that Alex makes is absolutely deadly.<br>I could tell such tales of his poisoning people.<br>Now, my dear, you give me that apron<br>And we’ll see what I can do. You stay and talk to Edward. |
| *(didascalia)* | [Exit [[characters/julia\|JULIA]]] |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | But what has happened, Edward? What has happened? |
| EDWARD | [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] is coming back, I think. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | You think! Don’t you know? |
| EDWARD | No, but I believe it. That man who was here — |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Yes, who was that man? I was rather afraid of him;<br><br><br>He has some sort of power. |
| EDWARD | I don’t know who he is.<br><br><br>But I had some talk with him, when the rest of you had left,<br>And he said he would bring [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] back, tomorrow. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | But why should that man want to bring her back —<br><br><br>Unless he is the Devil! I could believe he was. |
| EDWARD | Because I asked him to. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Because you asked him to!<br><br><br>Then he must be the Devil! He must have bewitched you.<br><br><br>How did he persuade you to want her back? |
| *(didascalia)* | [A popping noise is heard from the kitchen] |
| EDWARD | What the devil’s that? |
| *(didascalia)* | [Re-enter [[characters/julia\|JULIA]], in apron, with a tray and three glasses] |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | I’ve had an inspiration!<br><br><br>There’s nothing in the place fit to eat:<br>I’ve looked high and low. But I found some champagne —<br>Only a half-bottle, to be sure,<br>And of course it isn’t chilled. But it’s so refreshing;<br>And I thought, we are all in need of a stimulant<br>After this disaster. Now I’ll propose a health.<br>Can you guess whose health I’m going to propose? |
| EDWARD | No, I can’t. But I won’t drink to Alex’s. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | Oh, it isn’t Alex’s. Come, I give you<br><br><br>[[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]’s aunt! You might have guessed it.<br>EDWARD and [[characters/celia\|CELIA]]. [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]’s aunt. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | Now, the next question<br><br><br>Is, what’s to be done. That’s very simple.<br>It’s too late, or too early, to go to a restaurant.<br>You must both come [[motifs/home\|home]] with me. |
| EDWARD | No, I’m sorry, [[characters/julia\|Julia]].<br><br><br>I’m too tired to go out, and I’m not at all hungry.<br>I shall have a few biscuits. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | But you, [[characters/celia\|Celia]]?<br><br><br>You must come and have a light supper with me —<br>Something very light. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Thank you, [[characters/julia\|Julia]].<br><br><br>I think I will, if I may follow you<br>In about ten minutes? Before I go, there’s something<br>I want to say to Edward. |
| [[characters/julia\|JULIA]] | About [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]?<br><br><br>[[motifs/well\|Well]], come on quickly. And take a taxi.<br>You know, you’re looking absolutely famished.<br>Good [[motifs/night\|night]], Edward. |
| *(didascalia)* | [Exit [[characters/julia\|JULIA]]] |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | [[motifs/well\|Well]], how did he persuade you? |
| EDWARD | How did he persuade me? Did he persuade me?<br><br><br>I have a very clear impression<br>That he tried to persuade me it was all for the best<br>That [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] had gone; that I ought to be thankful.<br>And yet, the effect of all his argument<br>Was to make me see that I wanted her back. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | That’s the Devil’s method! So you want [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] back!<br><br><br>[[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]! So the one thing you care about<br>Is to avoid a break — anything unpleasant!<br>No, it can’t be that. I won’t think it’s that.<br>I think it is just a moment of surrender<br>To fatigue. And panic. You can’t face the trouble. |
| EDWARD | No, it is not that. It is not only that. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | It cannot be simply a question of [[concepts/vanity\|vanity]]:<br><br><br>That you think the world will laugh at you<br>Because your wife has left you for another man?<br>I shall soon put that right, Edward,<br>When you are free. |
| EDWARD | No, it is not that.<br><br><br>And all these reasons were suggested to me<br>By the man I call Riley — though his name is not Riley;<br>It was just a name in a [[Song\|song]] he sang … |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | He sang you a [[Song\|song]] about a man named Riley!<br><br><br>Really, Edward, I think you are mad —<br>I mean, you’re on the edge of a nervous breakdown.<br>Edward, if I go away now<br>Will you promise me to see a very great doctor<br>Whom I have heard of — and his name is Reilly! |
| EDWARD | It would need someone greater than the greatest doctor<br><br><br>To cure this illness. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Edward, if I go now,<br><br><br>Will you assure me that everything is right,<br>That you do not mean to have [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] back<br>And that you do mean to gain your [[concepts/freedom\|freedom]],<br>And that everything is all right between us?<br>That’s all that matters. Truly, Edward,<br>If that is right, everything else will be,<br>I promise you. |
| EDWARD | No, [[characters/celia\|Celia]].<br><br><br>It has been very wonderful, and I’m very grateful,<br>And I think you are a very rare person.<br>But it was too late. And I should have known<br>That it wasn’t fair to you. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | It wasn’t fair to me!<br><br><br>You can stand there and talk about being fair to me! |
| EDWARD | But for [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] leaving, this would never have arisen.<br><br><br>What future had you ever thought there could be? |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | What had I thought that the future could be?<br><br><br>I abandoned the future before we began,<br>And after that I lived in a present<br>Where [[motifs/time\|time]] was meaningless, a private world of ours,<br>Where the word ‘happiness’ had a different meaning<br>Or so it seemed. |
| EDWARD | I have heard of that experience. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | [[A Dream\|A dream]]. I was happy in it till to-day,<br><br><br>And then, when [[characters/julia\|Julia]] asked about [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]<br>And it came to me that [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]] had left you<br>And that you would be free — then I suddenly discovered<br>That the dream was not enough; that I wanted something more<br><br><br>And I waited, and wanted to run to tell you.<br>Perhaps the dream was better. It seemed the real reality,<br>And if this is reality, it is very like [[A Dream\|a dream]].<br>Perhaps it was I who betrayed my own dream<br>All the while; and to find I wanted<br>This world as [[motifs/well\|well]] as that … [[motifs/well\|well]], it’s humiliating. |
| EDWARD | There is no reason why you should feel humiliated … |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Oh, don’t think that you can humiliate me!<br><br><br>Humiliation — it’s something I’ve done to myself.<br>I am not sure even that you seem real enough<br>To humiliate me. I suppose that most women<br>Would feel degraded to find that a man<br>With whom they thought they had shared something wonderful<br>Had taken them only as a passing diversion.<br>Oh, I dare say that you deceived yourself:<br>But that’s what it was, no [[concepts/doubt\|doubt]]. |
| EDWARD | I didn’t take you as a passing diversion!<br><br><br>If you want to speak of passing diversions<br>How did you take Peter? |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Peter? Peter who? |
| EDWARD | Peter Quilpe, who was here this evening. He was in [[A Dream\|a dream]]<br><br><br>And now he is simply unhappy and bewildered. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | I simply don’t know what you are talking about.<br><br><br>Edward, this is really too crude a subterfuge<br>To justify yourself. There was never anything<br>Between me and Peter. |
| EDWARD | Wasn’t there? He thought so.<br><br><br>He came back this evening to talk to me about it. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | But this is ridiculous! I never gave Peter<br><br><br>Any reason to suppose I cared for him.<br>I thought he had talent; I saw that he was lonely;<br>I thought that I could help him. I took him to concerts.<br>But then, as he came to make more acquaintances,<br>I found him less interesting, and rather conceited.<br>But why should we talk about Peter? All that matters<br>Is, that you think you want [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]].<br>And if that is the sort of person you are —<br>[[motifs/well\|Well]], you had better have her. |
| EDWARD | It’s not like that.<br><br><br>It is not that I am in [[motifs/love\|love]] with [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]].<br>I don’t think I was ever really in [[motifs/love\|love]] with her.<br>If I have ever been in [[motifs/love\|love]] — and I think that I have —<br>I have never been in [[motifs/love\|love]] with anyone but you,<br>And perhaps I still am. But this can’t go on.<br>It never could have been … a permanent thing:<br>You should have a man … nearer your own age. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | I don’t think I care for advice from you, Edward:<br><br><br>You are not entitled to take any interest<br>Now, in my future. I only [[concepts/hope\|hope]] you’re competent<br><br><br>To manage your own. But if you are not in [[motifs/love\|love]]<br>And never have been in [[motifs/love\|love]] with [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]],<br>What is it that you want? |
| EDWARD | I am not sure.<br><br><br>The one thing of which I am relatively certain<br>Is, that only since this morning<br>I have met myself as a middle-aged man<br>Beginning to know what it is to feel old.<br>That is the worst moment, when you feel that you have lost<br>The [[motifs/desire\|desire]] for all that was most desirable,<br>Before you are contented with what you can [[motifs/desire\|desire]];<br>Before you know what is left to be desired;<br>And you go on wishing that you could [[motifs/desire\|desire]]<br>What [[motifs/desire\|desire]] has left behind. But you cannot understand.<br>How could you understand what it is to feel old? |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | But I want to understand you. I could understand.<br><br><br>And, Edward, please believe that whatever happens<br>I shall not loathe you. I shall only feel sorry for you.<br>It’s only myself I am in danger of loathing.<br>But what will your life be? I cannot bear to think of it.<br>Oh, Edward! Can you be happy with [[characters/lavinia\|Lavinia]]? |
| EDWARD | No — not happy: or, if there is any happiness,<br><br><br>Only the happiness of knowing<br>That the misery does not feed on the ruin of loveliness,<br>That the tedium is not the residue of ecstasy.<br>I see that my life was determined long ago<br>And that the struggle to escape from it<br>Is only a make-believe, a pretence<br>That what is, is not, or could be changed.<br>The [[concepts/self\|self]] that can say ‘I want this — or want that’ —<br>The [[concepts/self\|self]] that wills — he is a feeble creature;<br>He has to come to terms in the end<br>With the obstinate, the tougher [[concepts/self\|self]]; who does not speak,<br>Who never talks, who cannot argue;<br>And who in some men may be the guardian —<br><br><br>But in men like me, the dull, the implacable,<br>The indomitable spirit of mediocrity.<br>The willing [[concepts/self\|self]] can contrive the disaster<br>Of this unwilling partnership — but can only flourish<br>In submission to the rule of the stronger partner. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | I am not sure, Edward, that I understand you;<br><br><br>And yet I understand as I never did before.<br>I think — I believe — you are being yourself<br>As you never were before, with me.<br>Twice you have changed since I have been looking at you.<br>I looked at your face: and I thought that I knew<br>And loved every contour; and as I looked<br>It withered, as if I had unwrapped a mummy.<br>I listened to your voice, that had always thrilled me,<br>And it became another voice — no, not a voice:<br>What I heard was only the noise of an insect,<br>Dry, endless, meaningless, inhuman —<br>You might have made it by scraping your legs together —<br>Or however grasshoppers do it. I looked,<br>And listened for your heart, your [[motifs/blood\|blood]];<br>And saw only a beetle the size of a man<br>With nothing more inside it than what comes out<br>When you tread on a beetle. |
| EDWARD | Perhaps that is what I am.<br><br><br>Tread on me, if you like. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | No, I won’t tread on you.<br><br><br>That is not what you are. It is only what was left<br>Of what I had thought you were. I see another person,<br>I see you as a person whom I never saw before.<br>The man I saw before, he was only a projection —<br>I see that now — of something that I wanted —<br>No, not wanted — something I aspired to —<br><br><br>Something that I desperately wanted to exist.<br>It must happen somewhere — but what, and where is it?<br>Edward, I see that I was simply making use of you.<br>And I ask you to forgive me. |
| EDWARD | You … ask me to forgive you! |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Yes, for two things. First … |
| *(didascalia)* | [The telephone rings] |
| EDWARD | Damn the telephone.<br><br><br>I suppose I had better answer it. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Yes, better answer it. |
| EDWARD | Hello! … Oh, [[characters/julia\|Julia]]: what is it now?<br><br><br>Your spectacles again … where did you leave them?<br>Or have we … have I got to hunt all over?<br>Have you looked in your bag? … [[motifs/well\|Well]], don’t snap my head off …<br>You’re sure, in the kitchen? Beside the champagne bottle?<br>You’re quite sure? … Very [[motifs/well\|well]], hold on if you like;<br>We … I’ll look for them. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | Yes, you look for them.<br><br><br>I shall never go into your kitchen again. |
| *(didascalia)* | [Exit EDWARD. He returns with the spectacles and a bottle] |
| EDWARD | She was right for once. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | She is always right.<br><br><br>But why bring an empty champagne bottle? |
| EDWARD | It isn’t empty. It may be a little flat —<br><br><br>But why did she say that it was a half-bottle?<br>It’s one of my best: and I have no half-bottles.<br>[[motifs/well\|Well]], I hoped that you would drink a final glass with me. |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | What should we drink to? |
| EDWARD | Whom shall we drink to? |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | To the Guardians. |
| EDWARD | To the Guardians? |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | To the Guardians. It was you who spoke of guardians. |
| *(didascalia)* | [They drink] |
| [[characters/celia\|CELIA]] | It may be that even [[characters/julia\|Julia]] is a guardian.<br>Perhaps she is my guardian. Give me the spectacles.<br><br><br>Good [[motifs/night\|night]], Edward. |
| EDWARD | Good [[motifs/night\|night]] … [[characters/celia\|Celia]]. |
| *(didascalia)* | [Exit [[characters/celia\|CELIA]]] |
| EDWARD |                                                                      Oh! |
| *(didascalia)* | [He snatches up the receiver] |
| EDWARD | Hello, [[characters/julia\|Julia]]! are you there? …<br>[[motifs/well\|Well]], I’m awfully sorry to have kept you waiting;<br>But we … I had to hunt for them … No, I found them.<br>… Yes, she’s bringing them now … Good [[motifs/night\|night]].<br>CURTAIN |
